Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the functional_analysis space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 446 and the convergence rate is O(463/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to functional_analysis theory, we decompose the problem into 446 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 463. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the functional_analysis structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 92. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
